Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any linear module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 320. The projective resolution has length at most 470 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on linear structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 320. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 470. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The linear operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 456. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 563 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the linear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^456 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 563-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
